Creates canonical publication record (status='processing'), delegates to provider, updates to succeeded/failed, performs legacy shared_posts compatibility write for primary non-reply posts, returns result with publication_id.\n  - `enqueue(request: SocialPublishRequest) -> SocialPublishResult`: Creates canonical record with status='queued' and scheduled_at, returns result with publication_id (no provider call yet).\n  - `execute_publication(publication_id)`: Internal method used by scheduler to execute a claimed publication.\n  - `delete_publication(publication_id)`: Loads canonical record, validates delete_supported, delegates to provider.\n  - Success path: persist canonical status \u2192 optional legacy shared_posts write \u2192 return result with publication_id + provider refs + delete_supported + announcement data.\n\n- Create `src/features/sharing/providers/__init__.py` and `src/features/sharing/providers/x_provider.py` \u2014 Provider interface and X implementation:\n  - Interface: `publish(request)`, `delete(publication)`, `normalize_target_ref(...)`\n  - X provider wraps existing `social_poster.post_tweet()` and `social_poster.delete_tweet()` \n  - Supports actions: post, reply, retweet. Delete supported for post and reply only; retweet explicitly marked delete_supported=False.\n\n- Modify `src/features/sharing/sharer.py` \u2014 Refactor `Sharer` to be request-building and orchestration code:\n  - Keep media download, title extraction, caption generation in Sharer\n  - `finalize_sharing()` builds a `SocialPublishRequest` and hands it to `SocialPublishService.publish_now()` instead of calling `post_tweet()` directly\n  - Move duplicate detection from in-memory `_successfully_shared` set to canonical DB lookups that are action-aware and caller-aware (primary non-reply posts dedupe, replies don't, reaction bridge doesn't inherit admin duplicate suppression)\n  - `send_tweet()` similarly routes through the service\n\n**Critical behavioral preservation:**\n- Admin replies default to text_only=True (callers-2 flag)\n- Reaction bridge does NOT inherit duplicate suppression (callers-3 flag)\n- First-share notification now receives publication_id from result",

  "meta_commentary": "**Execution guidance:**\n\nThis is a large, multi-phase feature. The dependency chain is strictly linear (T1\u2192T2\u2192T3\u2192T4\u2192T5\u2192T6\u2192T7) because each layer builds on the previous. Do not skip ahead.\n\n**Key gotchas:**\n\n1. **RPC is new to this codebase.** The `claim_due_social_publications` Postgres function must be deployed to Supabase before the scheduler works. The SQL file is a checked-in artifact but deploying it is manual. Flag this to the operator after T1.\n\n2. **main.py loads SharingCog directly** \u2014 not via `bot.load_extension()`. This means `setup()` is NOT the active path. The worker loop MUST attach to `cog_load`/`cog_unload` lifecycle hooks. Verify by reading main.py lines ~137-143.\n\n3. **Three distinct caller paths must be preserved:** admin chat (text_only=True for replies, duplicate suppression), reaction bridge (moderation + consent + DMs, NO duplicate suppression), and summary-triggered (opt-out check). Each builds its own SocialPublishRequest \u2014 do NOT force them through a single parameter surface.\n\n4. **shared_posts compatibility is narrow:** Only write a legacy row for primary non-reply posts. Do NOT write one row per post|reply|retweet. The canonical source of truth is social_publications.\n\n5. **The admin query whitelist has TWO locations** in tools.py (~line 66 definition, ~line 1637 usage) plus the agent.py system prompt (~line 38). Missing any one makes the new tables invisible.\n\n6. **No existing test infrastructure.** You'll need to create the tests/ directory and likely add pytest to requirements. Keep tests focused \u2014 mock Supabase and Discord, test the service logic and caller wiring.\n\n7. **Route management is out of scope for v1.** Create the social_channel_routes table but don't build admin CRUD. Guild-default routing only.",

  request_payload jsonb not null default '{}'::jsonb,\n+    target_post_ref text,\n+    scheduled_at timestamptz not null default timezone('utc', now()),\n+    status text not null default 'queued' last_error text,\n+    provider_ref text,\n+    provider_url text,\n+    delete_supported boolean not null default false,\n+    created_at timestamptz not null default timezone('utc', now()),\n+    updated_at timestamptz not null default timezone('utc', now()),\n+    completed_at timestamptz,\n+    deleted_at timestamptz\n+);\n+\n+create index if not exists idx_social_publications_guild_status\n+    on public.social_publications (guild_id, status, scheduled_at);\n+\n+create index if not exists idx_social_publications_message_platform\n+    on public.social_publications (message_id, platform, action);\n+\n+create index if not exists idx_social_publications_due_queue\n+    on public.social_publications (scheduled_at, created_at)\n+    where status = 'queued' and deleted_at is null;\n+\n+drop trigger if exists social_publications_set_updated_at on public.social_publications;\n+create trigger social_publications_set_updated_at\n+before update on public.social_publications\n+for each row\n+execute function public.set_social_updated_at();\n+\n+create table if not exists public.social_channel_routes (\n+    id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),\n+    guild_id bigint not null,\n+    channel_id bigint,\n+    platform text not null,\n+    route_config jsonb not null default '{}'::jsonb,\n+    enabled boolean not null default true,\n+    created_at timestamptz not null default timezone('utc', now()),\n+    updated_at timestamptz not null default timezone('utc', now())\n+);\n+\n+create unique index if not exists uq_social_channel_routes_guild_default\n+    on public.social_channel_routes (guild_id, platform)\n+    where channel_id is null;\n+\n+create unique index if not exists uq_social_channel_routes_channel\n+    on public.social_channel_routes (guild_id, channel_id, platform)\n+    where channel_id is not null;\n+\n+drop trigger if exists function public.set_social_updated_at();\n+\n+create or replace function public.claim_due_social_publications(\n+    claim_limit integer default 10,\n+    claim_guild_ids bigint[] default null\n+)\n+returns setof public.social_publications\n+language plpgsql\n+as $$\n+begin\n+    return query\n+    with due_rows as (\n+        select sp.publication_id\n+        from public.social_publications sp\n+        where sp.status = 'queued'\n+          and sp.deleted_at is null\n+          and sp.scheduled_at <= timezone('utc', now())\n+          and (sp.retry_after is null or sp.retry_after <= timezone('utc', now()))\n+          and (claim_guild_ids is null or sp.guild_id = any(claim_guild_ids))\n+        order by coalesce(sp.retry_after, sp.scheduled_at), sp.created_at\n+        for update skip locked\n+        limit greatest(claim_limit, 0)\n+    ),\n+    updated_rows as (\n+        update public.social_publications sp\n+        set status = 'processing',\n+            attempt_count = coalesce(sp.attempt_count, 0) + 1,\n+            retry_after = null,\n+            updated_at = timezone('utc', now())\n+        from due_rows\n+        where sp.publication_id = due_rows.publication_id\n+        returning sp.*\n+    )\n+    select * from updated_rows;\n+end;\n+$$;\n*** End Patch\n"

'last_error': None,\n+                'updated_at': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n+            }\n+            if attempt_count is not None:\n+                payload['attempt_count'] = attempt_count\n+            if retry_after is not None:\n+                payload['retry_after'] = retry_after\n+            (\n+                self.supabase.table('social_publications')\n+                .update(self._serialize_supabase_value(payload))\n+                .eq('publication_id', publication_id)\n+                .eq('guild_id', effective_guild_id)\n+                .execute()\n+            )\n+            return True\n+        except Exception as e:\n+            logger.error(f\"Error marking social publication {publication_id} processing: {e}\", exc_info=True)\n+            return False\n+\n+    def mark_social_publication_succeeded(\n+        self,\n+        publication_id: str,\n+        guild_id: Optional[int] = None,\n+        provider_ref: Optional[str] = None,\n+        provider_url: Optional[str] = None,\n+        delete_supported: Optional[bool] = None\n+    ) -> bool:\n+        \"\"\"Mark a publication as successfully completed.\"\"\"\n+        effective_guild_id = guild_id or self._resolve_social_publication_guild_id(publication_id)\n+        if not self._gate_check(effective_guild_id):\n+            return False\n+        if not self.supabase:\n+            return False\n+\n+        try:\n+            payload: Dict[str, Any] = {\n+                'status': 'succeeded',\n+                'completed_at': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n+                'updated_at': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n+                'last_error': None,\n+                'retry_after': None,\n+            }\n+            if provider_ref is not None:\n+                payload['provider_ref'] = provider_ref\n+            if provider_url is not None:\n+                payload['provider_url'] = provider_url\n+            if delete_supported is not None:\n+                payload['delete_supported'] = delete_supported\n+            (\n+                self.supabase.table('social_publications')\n+                .update(payload)\n+                .eq('publication_id', publication_id)\n+                .eq('guild_id', effective_guild_id)\n+                .execute()\n+            )\n+            return True\n+        except Exception as e:\n+            logger.error(f\"Error marking social publication {publication_id} succeeded: {e}\", exc_info=True)\n+            return False\n+\n+    def mark_social_publication_failed(\n+        self,\n+        publication_id: str,\n+        last_error: str,\n+        guild_id: Optional[int] = None,\n+        retry_after: Optional[datetime] = None\n+    ) -> bool:\n+        \"\"\"Mark a publication as failed.\"\"\"\n+        effective_guild_id = guild_id or self._resolve_social_publication_guild_id(publication_id)\n+        if not self._gate_check(effective_guild_id):\n+            return False\n+        if not self.supabase:\n+            return False\n+\n+        try:\n+            payload: Dict[str, Any] = {\n+                'status': 'failed',\n+                'last_error': last_error,\n+                'completed_at': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n+                'updated_at': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n+            }\n+            if retry_after is not None:\n+                payload['retry_after'] = retry_after\n+            (\n+                self.supabase.table('social_publications')\n+                .update(self._serialize_supabase_value(payload))\n+                .eq('publication_id', publication_id)\n+                .eq('guild_id', effective_guild_id)\n+                .execute()\n+            )\n+            return True\n+        except Exception as e:\n+            logger.error(f\"Error marking social publication {publication_id} failed: {e}\", exc_info=True)\n+            return False\n+\n+    def mark_social_publication_cancelled(\n+        self,\n+        publication_id: str,\n+        guild_id: Optional[int] = None,\n+        last_error: Optional[str] = None\n+    ) -> bool:\n+        \"\"\"Mark a publication as cancelled.\"\"\"\n+        effective_guild_id = guild_id or self._resolve_social_publication_guild_id(publication_id)\n+        if not self._gate_check(effective_guild_id):\n+            return False\n+        if not self.supabase:\n+            return False\n+\n+        try:\n+            payload: Dict[str, Any] = {\n+                'status': 'cancelled',\n+                'completed_at': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n+                'updated_at': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n+                'retry_after': None,\n+            }\n+            if last_error is not None:\n+                payload['last_error'] = last_error\n+            (\n+                self.supabase.table('social_publications')\n+                .update(payload)\n+                .eq('publication_id', publication_id)\n+                .eq('guild_id', effective_guild_id)\n+                .execute()\n+            )\n+            return True\n+        except Exception as e:\n+            logger.error(f\"Error marking social publication {publication_id} cancelled: {e}\", exc_info=True)\n+            return False\n+\n+    def claim_due_social_publications(\n+        self,\n+        limit: int = 10,\n+        guild_ids: Optional[List[int]] = None\n+    ) -> List[Dict]:\n+        \"\"\"Atomically claim due queued publications through the Supabase RPC.\"\"\"\n+        if not self.supabase:\n+            return []\n+\n+        writable_guild_ids = guild_ids\n+        if writable_guild_ids is None and self.server_config:\n+            writable_guild_ids = [\n+                server['guild_id']\n+                for server in self.server_config.get_enabled_servers(require_write=True)\n+                if server.get('guild_id') is not None\n+            ]\n+        if writable_guild_ids == []:\n+            return []\n+\n+        try:\n+            params: Dict[str, Any] = {'claim_limit': limit}\n+            if writable_guild_ids is not None:\n+                params['claim_guild_ids'] = writable_guild_ids\n+            result = self.supabase.rpc('claim_due_social_publications', params).execute()\n+            return result.data or []\n+        except Exception as e:\n+            logger.error(f\"Error claiming due social publications: {e}\", exc_info=True)\n+            return []\n \n     def mark_member_first_shared(self, member_id: int,         Idea:
Implement a unified social publishing system that preserves current immediate Twitter/X posting, adds scheduled queueing for tweets/replies/retweets, tracks success and failure, and is extensible to multiple outbound channels/platforms with per-channel routing and configuration.

        Batch framing:
        - Execute batch 3 of 7.
        - Actionable task IDs for this batch: ['T3']
        - Already completed task IDs available as dependency context: ['T1', 'T2']

        Actionable tasks for this batch:
        [
  {
    "id": "T3",
    "description": "Introduce SocialPublishService with publish_now/enqueue entrypoints and wrap X behind a provider interface.\n\n**Files to create/modify:**\n- Create `src/features/sharing/social_publish_service.py` \u2014 Central service with:\n  - `publish_now(request: SocialPublishRequest) -> SocialPublishResult`: Immediate execution path. Creates canonical publication record (status='processing'), delegates to provider, updates to succeeded/failed, performs legacy shared_posts compatibility write for primary non-reply posts, returns result with publication_id.\n  - `enqueue(request: SocialPublishRequest) -> SocialPublishResult`: Creates canonical record with status='queued' and scheduled_at, returns result with publication_id (no provider call yet).\n  - `execute_publication(publication_id)`: Internal method used by scheduler to execute a claimed publication.\n  - `delete_publication(publication_id)`: Loads canonical record, validates delete_supported, delegates to provider.\n  - Success path: persist canonical status \u2192 optional legacy shared_posts write \u2192 return result with publication_id + provider refs + delete_supported + announcement data.\n\n- Create `src/features/sharing/providers/__init__.py` and `src/features/sharing/providers/x_provider.py` \u2014 Provider interface and X implementation:\n  - Interface: `publish(request)`, `delete(publication)`, `normalize_target_ref(...)`\n  - X provider wraps existing `social_poster.post_tweet()` and `social_poster.delete_tweet()` \n  - Supports actions: post, reply, retweet. Delete supported for post and reply only; retweet explicitly marked delete_supported=False.\n\n- Modify `src/features/sharing/sharer.py` \u2014 Refactor `Sharer` to be request-building and orchestration code:\n  - Keep media download, title extraction, caption generation in Sharer\n  - `finalize_sharing()` builds a `SocialPublishRequest` and hands it to `SocialPublishService.publish_now()` instead of calling `post_tweet()` directly\n  - Move duplicate detection from in-memory `_successfully_shared` set to canonical DB lookups that are action-aware and caller-aware (primary non-reply posts dedupe, replies don't, reaction bridge doesn't inherit admin duplicate suppression)\n  - `send_tweet()` similarly routes through the service\n\n**Critical behavioral preservation:**\n- Admin replies default to text_only=True (callers-2 flag)\n- Reaction bridge does NOT inherit duplicate suppression (callers-3 flag)\n- First-share notification now receives publication_id from result",

Include the `claim_due_social_publications` Postgres function that atomically selects and updates due rows (status='queued', scheduled_at <= now()) to status='processing' using `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`, returning claimed rows.\n- Modify `src/common/db_handler.py` \u2014 Add methods using **fluent Supabase client** (NOT raw SQL) for all CRUD: `create_social_publication()`, `get_social_publication_by_id()`, `get_social_publications_for_message()`, `list_social_publications()`, `mark_social_publication_processing()`, `mark_social_publication_succeeded()`, `mark_social_publication_failed()`, `mark_social_publication_cancelled()`. Add one RPC-only method: `claim_due_social_publications()` using `self.supabase.rpc('claim_due_social_publications', {...}).execute()`. Do NOT use raw SQL via execute_query for this subsystem.\n\n**Settled decisions:**\n- Fluent client for CRUD, RPC only for atomic claim (settled decision `db-access-pattern`)\n- `shared_posts` stays for legacy consumers; do not write one legacy row per post|reply|retweet outcome \u2014 only write a legacy row for primary non-reply posts where existing callers depend on it\n- The SQL file is a checked-in artifact; deploying it to Supabase is an ops step (see watch items)\n\n**DB method signatures should mirror existing patterns** \u2014 see `record_shared_post()` at db_handler.py:883 for style reference (guild_id gating via `_gate_check`, try/except with logging, return bool or Optional[Dict]).",

    "[DEBT] db-access-pattern: Use fluent Supabase client for all CRUD on social_publications. RPC ONLY for claim_due_social_publications. Do NOT introduce raw SQL via execute_query. Settled decision.",
    "[DEBT] supabase-rpc-prerequisite: The claim_due_social_publications SQL function must be deployed to Supabase dashboard BEFORE the scheduler can run. This is a first-time RPC usage in the codebase. Check sql/social_publications.sql for the function definition and deploy it manually.",
    "[DEBT] route-management: social_channel_routes table ships empty. V1 uses guild-default routing only. Channel-specific routes require manual DB inserts. No admin CRUD commands for routes in this release.",

    "[BEHAVIORAL] first-share-notification: send_post_share_notification must receive publication_id from the service result. Delete button resolves one canonical record, not inferred from discord_message_id + tweet_id.",

  "meta_commentary": "**Execution guidance:**\n\nThis is a large, multi-phase feature. The dependency chain is strictly linear (T1\u2192T2\u2192T3\u2192T4\u2192T5\u2192T6\u2192T7) because each layer builds on the previous. Do not skip ahead.\n\n**Key gotchas:**\n\n1. **RPC is new to this codebase.** The `claim_due_social_publications` Postgres function must be deployed to Supabase before the scheduler works. The SQL file is a checked-in artifact but deploying it is manual. Flag this to the operator after T1.\n\n2. **main.py loads SharingCog directly** \u2014 not via `bot.load_extension()`. This means `setup()` is NOT the active path. The worker loop MUST attach to `cog_load`/`cog_unload` lifecycle hooks. Verify by reading main.py lines ~137-143.\n\n3. **Three distinct caller paths must be preserved:** admin chat (text_only=True for replies, duplicate suppression), reaction bridge (moderation + consent + DMs, NO duplicate suppression), and summary-triggered (opt-out check). Each builds its own SocialPublishRequest \u2014 do NOT force them through a single parameter surface.\n\n4. **shared_posts compatibility is narrow:** Only write a legacy row for primary non-reply posts. request.target_post_ref,\n                'provider_url': 'https://twitter.com/example/status/' + str(request.target_post_ref),\n                'tweet_id': request.target_post_ref,\n                'tweet_url': 'https://twitter.com/example/status/' + str(request.target_post_ref),\n                'delete_supported': False,\n            }\n        return {\n            'provider_ref': create_social_publication(self, data, guild_id=None):\n        publication_id = f\"pub-{len(self.rows) + 1}\"\n        row = dict(data)\n        row['publication_id'] = publication_id\n        self.rows[publication_id] = row\n        return row\n    def mark_social_publication_succeeded(self, publication_id, guild_id=None, provider_ref=None, provider_url=None, delete_supported=None):\n        self.rows[publication_id].update({\n            'status': 'succeeded',\n            'provider_ref': provider_ref,\n            'provider_url': provider_url,\n            'delete_supported': delete_supported,\n        })\n        return True\n    def mark_social_publication_failed(self, publication_id, last_error, guild_id=None, retry_after=None):\n        self.rows[publication_id].update({'status': 'failed', 'last_error': last_error})\n        return True\n    def mark_social_publication_processing(self, publication_id, guild_id=None, attempt_count=None, retry_after=None):\n        self.rows[publication_id].update({'status': 'processing'})\n        return True\n    def mark_social_publication_cancelled(self, publication_id, guild_id=None, last_error=None):\n        self.rows[publication_id].update({'status': 'cancelled'})\n        return True\n    def get_social_publication_by_id(self, publication_id, guild_id=None):\n        return self.rows.get(publication_id)\n    def record_shared_post(self, **kwargs):\n        self.shared_posts.append(kwargs)\n        return True\n    def mark_shared_post_deleted(self, *args, **kwargs):\n        return True\n\nasync def main():\n    service = SocialPublishService(FakeDB(), providers={'twitter': FakeProvider()}, logger_instance=None)\n    request = SocialPublishRequest(\n        message_id=1,\n        channel_id=2,\n        guild_id=3,\n        user_id=4,\n        platform='twitter',\n        text='hello',\n        source_kind='admin_chat',\n        source_context=PublicationSourceContext(source_kind='admin_chat', metadata={'user_details': {'username': 'u'}}),\n    )\n    result = await service.publish_now(request)\n    assert result.success and result.publication_id and result.delete_supported is True\n    retweet = SocialPublishRequest(\n        message_id=5,\n        channel_id=6,\n        guild_id=3,\n        user_id=4,\n        platform='twitter',\n        action='retweet',\n        target_post_ref='999',\n        source_kind='admin_chat',\n        source_context=PublicationSourceContext(source_kind='admin_chat', metadata={'user_details': {'username': 'u'}}),\n    )\n    retweet_result = await service.publish_now(retweet)\n    assert retweet_result.success and retweet_result.delete_supported is False\n    print('ok')\n\nasyncio.run(main())\nPY",

> AGENT

The verification uncovered one useful compatibility improvement: `social_publish_service.py` was importing `DatabaseHandler` and `XProvider` eagerly even when callers passed a fake provider. I’m making those imports lazy/type-only so the module can be imported cleanly on the repo’s default interpreter and in tests.

  self.rows[publication_id].update({'status': 'succeeded', 'provider_ref': provider_ref, 'provider_url': provider_url, 'delete_supported': delete_supported})\n        return True\n    def mark_social_publication_failed(self, publication_id, last_error, guild_id=None, retry_after=None):\n        self.rows[publication_id].update({'status': 'failed', 'last_error': last_error})\n        return True\n    def mark_social_publication_processing(self, publication_id, guild_id=None, attempt_count=None, retry_after=None):\n        self.rows[publication_id].update({'status': Include the `claim_due_social_publications` Postgres function that atomically selects and updates due rows (status='queued', scheduled_at <= now()) to status='processing' using `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`, returning claimed rows.\n- Modify `src/common/db_handler.py` \u2014 Add methods using **fluent Supabase client** (NOT raw SQL) for all CRUD: `create_social_publication()`, `get_social_publication_by_id()`, `get_social_publications_for_message()`, `list_social_publications()`, `mark_social_publication_processing()`, `mark_social_publication_succeeded()`, `mark_social_publication_failed()`, `mark_social_publication_cancelled()`. Add one RPC-only method: `claim_due_social_publications()` using `self.supabase.rpc('claim_due_social_publications', {...}).execute()`. Do NOT use raw SQL via execute_query for this subsystem.\n\n**Settled decisions:**\n- Fluent client for CRUD, RPC only for atomic claim (settled decision `db-access-pattern`)\n- `shared_posts` stays for legacy consumers; do not write one legacy row per post|reply|retweet outcome \u2014 only write a legacy row for primary non-reply posts where existing callers depend on it\n- The SQL file is a checked-in artifact; deploying it to Supabase is an ops step (see watch items)\n\n**DB method signatures should mirror existing patterns** \u2014 see `record_shared_post()` at db_handler.py:883 for style reference (guild_id gating via `_gate_check`, try/except with logging, return bool or Optional[Dict]).",

    "id": "T3",
    "description": "Introduce SocialPublishService with publish_now/enqueue entrypoints and wrap X behind a provider interface.\n\n**Files to create/modify:**\n- Create `src/features/sharing/social_publish_service.py` \u2014 Central service with:\n  - `publish_now(request: SocialPublishRequest) -> SocialPublishResult`: Immediate execution path. Creates canonical publication record (status='processing'), delegates to provider, updates to succeeded/failed, performs legacy shared_posts compatibility write for primary non-reply posts, returns result with publication_id.\n  - `enqueue(request: SocialPublishRequest) -> SocialPublishResult`: Creates canonical record with status='queued' and scheduled_at, returns result with publication_id (no provider call yet).\n  - `execute_publication(publication_id)`: Internal method used by scheduler to execute a claimed publication.\n  - `delete_publication(publication_id)`: Loads canonical record, validates delete_supported, delegates to provider.\n  - Success path: persist canonical status \u2192 optional legacy shared_posts write \u2192 return result with publication_id + provider refs + delete_supported + announcement data.\n\n- Create `src/features/sharing/providers/__init__.py` and `src/features/sharing/providers/x_provider.py` \u2014 Provider interface and X implementation:\n  - Interface: `publish(request)`, `delete(publication)`, `normalize_target_ref(...)`\n  - X provider wraps existing `social_poster.post_tweet()` and `social_poster.delete_tweet()` \n  - Supports actions: post, reply, retweet. Delete supported for post and reply only; retweet explicitly marked delete_supported=False.\n\n- Modify `src/features/sharing/sharer.py` \u2014 Refactor `Sharer` to be request-building and orchestration code:\n  - Keep media download, title extraction, caption generation in Sharer\n  - `finalize_sharing()` builds a `SocialPublishRequest` and hands it to `SocialPublishService.publish_now()` instead of calling `post_tweet()` directly\n  - Move duplicate detection from in-memory `_successfully_shared` set to canonical DB lookups that are action-aware and caller-aware (primary non-reply posts dedupe, replies don't, reaction bridge doesn't inherit admin duplicate suppression)\n  - `send_tweet()` similarly routes through the service\n\n**Critical behavioral preservation:**\n- Admin replies default to text_only=True (callers-2 flag)\n- Reaction bridge does NOT inherit duplicate suppression (callers-3 flag)\n- First-share notification now receives publication_id from result",

        Idea:
Implement a unified social publishing system that preserves current immediate Twitter/X posting, adds scheduled queueing for tweets/replies/retweets, tracks success and failure, and is extensible to multiple outbound channels/platforms with per-channel routing and configuration.

        Batch framing:
        - Execute batch 5 of 7.
        - Actionable task IDs for this batch: ['T5']
        - Already completed task IDs available as dependency context: ['T1', 'T2', 'T3', 'T4']

  {
    "id": "T5",
    "description": "Add route resolution and scheduled worker lifecycle on the real load path.\n\n**Files to create/modify:**\n\n1. `src/common/server_config.py` \u2014 Add route resolution helper:\n   - `resolve_social_route(guild_id, channel_id, platform)`: Requires sharing_enabled via is_feature_enabled(), then resolves: exact channel route \u2192 parent channel route \u2192 guild default route\n   - For v1, guild-default routing is the only active path (settled decision `v1-routing-scope`); channel-specific routes can be seeded via direct DB inserts\n   - Explicit route/platform override only where admin tool provides one\n\n2. `src/features/sharing/sharing_cog.py` \u2014 Add scheduled worker:\n   - Use `discord.ext.tasks.loop` pattern (consistent with repo conventions)\n   - Worker runs on interval (e.g., 30s), calls db_handler.claim_due_social_publications(), executes each through SocialPublishService.execute_publication()\n   - **Critical: main.py instantiates SharingCog directly** (`SharingCog(bot, bot.db_handler)` then `await bot.add_cog(...)`) \u2014 worker startup MUST use cog_load/cog_unload lifecycle hooks, NOT rely on module-level setup() alone\n   - before_loop: wait_until_ready() gating\n   - cog_load: start the loop\n   - cog_unload: cancel/stop the loop\n   - Keep setup() aligned with direct-instantiation path for compatibility\n   - Bounded retries for transient provider failures; persist terminal states with publication_id, platform, action, and provider error in logs\n   - Normalize scheduled times to UTC before persistence; no natural-language time parsing\n\n3. `main.py` \u2014 Wire SocialPublishService into SharingCog:\n   - SharingCog needs access to SocialPublishService (constructed with db_handler and X provider)\n   - Ensure the service instance is accessible to admin tools and sharer via bot or cog reference",

      "T4"

        [
  {
    "id": "T1",
    "description": "Create SQL artifact and DatabaseHandler helpers for canonical publication storage.\n\n**Files to create/modify:**\n- Create `sql/social_publications.sql` \u2014 DDL for `social_publications` table (publication_id UUID PK, guild_id, channel_id, message_id, user_id, source_kind, platform, action [post|reply|retweet], route_key, request_payload JSONB, target_post_ref, scheduled_at timestamptz, status [queued|processing|succeeded|failed|cancelled], attempt_count, retry_after timestamptz, last_error text, provider_ref, provider_url, delete_supported bool, created_at, updated_at, completed_at, deleted_at) and `social_channel_routes` table (id, guild_id, channel_id, platform, route_config JSONB, enabled bool, timestamps). Include the `claim_due_social_publications` Postgres function that atomically selects and updates due rows (status='queued', scheduled_at <= now()) to status='processing' using `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`, returning claimed rows.\n- Modify `src/common/db_handler.py` \u2014 Add methods using **fluent Supabase client** (NOT raw SQL) for all CRUD: `create_social_publication()`, `get_social_publication_by_id()`, `get_social_publications_for_message()`, `list_social_publications()`, `mark_social_publication_processing()`, `mark_social_publication_succeeded()`, `mark_social_publication_failed()`, `mark_social_publication_cancelled()`. Add one RPC-only method: `claim_due_social_publications()` using `self.supabase.rpc('claim_due_social_publications', {...}).execute()`. Do NOT use raw SQL via execute_query for this subsystem.\n\n**Settled decisions:**\n- Fluent client for CRUD, RPC only for atomic claim (settled decision `db-access-pattern`)\n- `shared_posts` stays for legacy consumers; do not write one legacy row per post|reply|retweet outcome \u2014 only write a legacy row for primary non-reply posts where existing callers depend on it\n- The SQL file is a checked-in artifact; deploying it to Supabase is an ops step (see watch items)\n\n**DB method signatures should mirror existing patterns** \u2014 see `record_shared_post()` at db_handler.py:883 for style reference (guild_id gating via `_gate_check`, try/except with logging, return bool or Optional[Dict]).",

    "id": "T3",
    "description": "Introduce SocialPublishService with publish_now/enqueue entrypoints and wrap X behind a provider interface.\n\n**Files to create/modify:**\n- Create `src/features/sharing/social_publish_service.py` \u2014 Central service with:\n  - `publish_now(request: SocialPublishRequest) -> SocialPublishResult`: Immediate execution path. Creates canonical publication record (status='processing'), delegates to provider, updates to succeeded/failed, performs legacy shared_posts compatibility write for primary non-reply posts, returns result with publication_id.\n  - `enqueue(request: SocialPublishRequest) -> SocialPublishResult`: Creates canonical record with status='queued' and scheduled_at, returns result with publication_id (no provider call yet).\n  - `execute_publication(publication_id)`: Internal method used by scheduler to execute a claimed publication.\n  - `delete_publication(publication_id)`: Loads canonical record, validates delete_supported, delegates to provider.\n  - Success path: persist canonical status \u2192 optional legacy shared_posts write \u2192 return result with publication_id + provider refs + delete_supported + announcement data.\n\n- Create `src/features/sharing/providers/__init__.py` and `src/features/sharing/providers/x_provider.py` \u2014 Provider interface and X implementation:\n  - Interface: `publish(request)`, `delete(publication)`, `normalize_target_ref(...)`\n  - X provider wraps existing `social_poster.post_tweet()` and `social_poster.delete_tweet()` \n  - Supports actions: post, reply, retweet. Delete supported for post and reply only; retweet explicitly marked delete_supported=False.\n\n- Modify `src/features/sharing/sharer.py` \u2014 Refactor `Sharer` to be request-building and orchestration code:\n  - Keep media download, title extraction, caption generation in Sharer\n  - `finalize_sharing()` builds a `SocialPublishRequest` and hands it to `SocialPublishService.publish_now()` instead of calling `post_tweet()` directly\n  - Move duplicate detection from in-memory `_successfully_shared` set to canonical DB lookups that are action-aware and caller-aware (primary non-reply posts dedupe, replies don't, reaction bridge doesn't inherit admin duplicate suppression)\n  - `send_tweet()` similarly routes through the service\n\n**Critical behavioral preservation:**\n- Admin replies default to text_only=True (callers-2 flag)\n- Reaction bridge does NOT inherit duplicate suppression (callers-3 flag)\n- First-share notification now receives publication_id from result",

      "id": "T3",
      "description": "Introduce SocialPublishService with publish_now/enqueue entrypoints and wrap X behind a provider interface.\n\n**Files to create/modify:**\n- Create `src/features/sharing/social_publish_service.py` \u2014 Central service with:\n  - `publish_now(request: SocialPublishRequest) -> SocialPublishResult`: Immediate execution path. Creates canonical publication record (status='processing'), delegates to provider, updates to succeeded/failed, performs legacy shared_posts compatibility write for primary non-reply posts, returns result with publication_id.\n  - `enqueue(request: SocialPublishRequest) -> SocialPublishResult`: Creates canonical record with status='queued' and scheduled_at, returns result with publication_id (no provider call yet).\n  - `execute_publication(publication_id)`: Internal method used by scheduler to execute a claimed publication.\n  - `delete_publication(publication_id)`: Loads canonical record, validates delete_supported, delegates to provider.\n  - Success path: persist canonical status \u2192 optional legacy shared_posts write \u2192 return result with publication_id + provider refs + delete_supported + announcement data.\n\n- Create `src/features/sharing/providers/__init__.py` and `src/features/sharing/providers/x_provider.py` \u2014 Provider interface and X implementation:\n  - Interface: `publish(request)`, `delete(publication)`, `normalize_target_ref(...)`\n  - X provider wraps existing `social_poster.post_tweet()` and `social_poster.delete_tweet()` \n  - Supports actions: post, reply, retweet. Delete supported for post and reply only; retweet explicitly marked delete_supported=False.\n\n- Modify `src/features/sharing/sharer.py` \u2014 Refactor `Sharer` to be request-building and orchestration code:\n  - Keep media download, title extraction, caption generation in Sharer\n  - `finalize_sharing()` builds a `SocialPublishRequest` and hands it to `SocialPublishService.publish_now()` instead of calling `post_tweet()` directly\n  - Move duplicate detection from in-memory `_successfully_shared` set to canonical DB lookups that are action-aware and caller-aware (primary non-reply posts dedupe, replies don't, reaction bridge doesn't inherit admin duplicate suppression)\n  - `send_tweet()` similarly routes through the service\n\n**Critical behavioral preservation:**\n- Admin replies default to text_only=True (callers-2 flag)\n- Reaction bridge does NOT inherit duplicate suppression (callers-3 flag)\n- First-share notification now receives publication_id from result",

      "description": "Add route resolution and scheduled worker lifecycle on the real load path.\n\n**Files to create/modify:**\n\n1. `src/common/server_config.py` \u2014 Add route resolution helper:\n   - `resolve_social_route(guild_id, channel_id, platform)`: Requires sharing_enabled via is_feature_enabled(), then resolves: exact channel route \u2192 parent channel route \u2192 guild default route\n   - For v1, guild-default routing is the only active path (settled decision `v1-routing-scope`); channel-specific routes can be seeded via direct DB inserts\n   - Explicit route/platform override only where admin tool provides one\n\n2. `src/features/sharing/sharing_cog.py` \u2014 Add scheduled worker:\n   - Use `discord.ext.tasks.loop` pattern (consistent with repo conventions)\n   - Worker runs on interval (e.g., 30s), calls db_handler.claim_due_social_publications(), executes each through SocialPublishService.execute_publication()\n   - **Critical: main.py instantiates SharingCog directly** (`SharingCog(bot, bot.db_handler)` then `await bot.add_cog(...)`) \u2014 worker startup MUST use cog_load/cog_unload lifecycle hooks, NOT rely on module-level setup() alone\n   - before_loop: wait_until_ready() gating\n   - cog_load: start the loop\n   - cog_unload: cancel/stop the loop\n   - Keep setup() aligned with direct-instantiation path for compatibility\n   - Bounded retries for transient provider failures; persist terminal states with publication_id, platform, action, and provider error in logs\n   - Normalize scheduled times to UTC before persistence; no natural-language time parsing\n\n3. `main.py` \u2014 Wire SocialPublishService into SharingCog:\n   - SharingCog needs access to SocialPublishService (constructed with db_handler and X provider)\n   - Ensure the service instance is accessible to admin tools and sharer via bot or cog reference",

publication.get('platform')\n+        action = publication.get('action')\n+        attempt_count = int(publication.get('attempt_count') or 0)\n+\n+        if not publication_id:\n+            logger.warning(\"[SharingCog] Claimed publication without publication_id; skipping.\")\n+            return\n+\n+        try:\n+            result = await self.social_publish_service.execute_publication(publication_id)\n+            if result.success:\n+                logger.info(\n+                    f\"[SharingCog] Publication {publication_id} succeeded \"\n+                    f\"(platform={platform}, action={action}).\"\n+                )\n+                return\n+\n+            error_message = result.error or \"Unknown publish failure\"\n+            if self._should_retry_publication(error_message, attempt_count):\n+                retry_after = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(\n+                    seconds=self.retry_delay_seconds * max(attempt_count, 1)\n+                )\n+                if self._requeue_publication(publication, error_message, retry_after):\n+                    logger.warning(\n+                        f\"[SharingCog] Requeued publication {publication_id} after transient failure \"\n+                        f\"(platform={platform}, action={action}, attempt={attempt_count}, \"\n+                        f\"retry_after={retry_after.isoformat()}, error={error_message}).\"\n+                    )\n+                    return\n+\n+            logger.error(\n+                f\"[SharingCog] Publication {publication_id} failed \"\n+                f\"(platform={platform}, action={action}, attempt={attempt_count}, error={error_message}).\"\n+            )\n+        except Exception as e:\n+            logger.error(\n+                f\"[SharingCog] Unexpected scheduler error for publication {publication_id} \"\n+                f\"(platform={platform}, action={action}): {e}\",\n+                exc_info=True,\n+            )\n+\n+    def _should_retry_publication(self, error_message: str, attempt_count: int) -> bool:\n+        \"\"\"Return True when a publish failure looks transient and retry budget remains.\"\"\"\n+        if attempt_count >= self.max_attempts:\n+            return False\n+\n+        normalized_error = (error_message or '').lower()\n+        transient_markers = (\n+            'provider publish failed',\n+            'timeout',\n+            'tempor',\n+            'try again',\n+            'rate limit',\n+            '429',\n+            '500',\n+            '502',\n+            '503',\n+            '504',\n+            'connection reset',\n+            'service unavailable',\n+        )\n+        return any(marker in normalized_error for marker in transient_markers)\n+\n+    def _requeue_publication(\n+        self,\n+        publication: dict,\n+        error_message: str,\n+        retry_after: datetime,\n+    ) -> bool:\n+        \"\"\"Move a failed processing row back to queued for another attempt.\"\"\"\n+        if not self.db_handler.supabase:\n+            return False\n+\n+        publication_id = publication.get('publication_id')\n+        guild_id = publication.get('guild_id')\n+        if not publication_id or guild_id is None:\n+            return False\n+\n+        try:\n+            self.db_handler.supabase.table('social_publications').update({\n+                'status': 'queued',\n+                'retry_after': retry_after.isoformat(),\n+                'last_error': error_message,\n+                'completed_at': None,\n+                'updated_at': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n+            }).eq('publication_id', publication_id).eq('guild_id', guild_id).execute()\n+            return True\n+        except Exception as e:\n+            logger.error(\n+                f\"[SharingCog] Failed to requeue publication {publication_id}: {e}\",\n+                exc_info=True,\n+            )\n+            return False\n+\n+\n+async def setup(bot: commands.Bot):\n+    if not hasattr(bot, 'db_handler'):\n+        logger.error(\"Database handler not found on bot object. Cannot load SharingCog.\")\n+        return\n+\n+    try:\n+        logger.info(\"About to create SharingCog instance...\")\n+        cog_instance = SharingCog(\n+            bot,\n+            bot.db_handler,\n+            social_publish_service=getattr(bot, 'social_publish_service', None),\n+        )\n+        logger.info(\"SharingCog instance created, adding to bot...\")\n+        await bot.add_cog(cog_instance)\n+        logger.info(\"SharingCog added to bot.\")\n+    except Exception as e:\n+        logger.error(f\"Error in SharingCog setup: {e}\")\n+        import traceback\n+        logger.error(f\"Traceback: {traceback.format_exc()}\")\n+        raise\n*** End Patch\n"
}

{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/brain-of-bndc/src/features/admin_chat/agent.py\n@@\n-**Finding things:**\n+**Finding things:**\n - find_messages — search/browse messages. Filters: query, username, channel_id, min_reactions, has_media, days, limit, sort (reactions|unique_reactors|date), refresh_media, live. Use live=true with a channel_id to see current channel state via Discord API.\n - inspect_message — full detail on one message: content, per-emoji reactions, context, replies, fresh media URLs.\n-- query_table — query any DB table with filters. Tables: competitions, competition_entries, discord_reactions, discord_messages, members, discord_channels, events, invite_codes, grant_applications, daily_summaries, shared_posts, pending_intros, intro_votes, timed_mutes. Filter operators: gt., gte., lt., lte., neq., like., ilike., in., is.null, not.null. Cheatsheet: discord_messages => author_id, created_at, reaction_count; shared_posts => discord_user_id, shared_at, platform.\n+- query_table — query any DB table with filters. Tables: competitions, competition_entries, discord_reactions, discord_messages, members, discord_channels, events, invite_codes, grant_applications, daily_summaries, shared_posts, social_publications, social_channel_routes, pending_intros, intro_votes, timed_mutes. Filter operators: gt., gte., lt., lte., neq., like., ilike., in., is.null, not.null. Cheatsheet: discord_messages => author_id, created_at, reaction_count; shared_posts => discord_user_id, shared_at, platform; social_publications => publication_id, status, platform, action, provider_ref, provider_url, last_error, scheduled_at.\n - get_active_channels, get_daily_summaries, get_member_info, get_bot_status, search_logs\n \n **Doing things:**\n - send_message(channel_id, content, reply_to?) — CDN URLs are auto-refreshed before sending.\n - edit_message(channel_id, message_id, content)\n - delete_message(channel_id, message_id?, message_ids?) — delete one or many messages. You can delete ANY message, not just your own. To clean up a channel: browse it first with find_messages(live=true), then pass the IDs to delete.\n - upload_file(channel_id, file_path, content?)\n-- share_to_social(message_id or message_link, tweet_text?, reply_to_tweet?, text_only?) — share to Twitter as a standalone post or thread reply. Tweets are free-form: the source Discord message does NOT need attachments, and `tweet_text` alone is enough to post text-only. If the source message has attachments they'll be included by default. `reply_to_tweet` accepts a tweet URL or ID. **Thread replies default to text-only** so a follow-up doesn't reattach the parent tweet's media — pass `text_only=false` explicitly if you DO want a reply to also include the source message's media. Success returns `tweet_url` and `tweet_id`; if `already_shared` is true on a rerun without `reply_to_tweet`, cite the returned `tweet_url` instead of treating it as a failure.\n+- share_to_social(message_id or message_link, tweet_text?, action?, target_post?, reply_to_tweet?, text_only?, schedule_for?, platform?) — share or schedule publishing to X/Twitter. Supports post, reply, and retweet actions. `schedule_for` must be ISO-8601 with timezone. `target_post` or `reply_to_tweet` accepts a tweet URL or ID. **Thread replies default to text-only** so a follow-up doesn't reattach the parent tweet's media — pass `text_only=false` explicitly if you DO want a reply to also include the source message's media. Immediate success returns `tweet_url`/`tweet_id` plus `publication_id`; scheduled success returns `publication_id` with queued status. If `already_shared` is true on a rerun, cite the returned existing `tweet_url` instead of treating it as a failure.\n - update_member_socials(user_id, twitter_url?, reddit_url?) — set/clear a member's Twitter or Reddit handle on file. The Twitter handle is what social picks use to auto-tag the creator. Pass an empty string to clear a field; omit a field to leave it alone. Use this whenever the admin says things like \"set X's twitter to @y\" or \"X is @y on twitter\".\n - resolve_user(username) — get a user's Discord ID and mention tag.\n@@\n-**Media.** When the user asks for a video, image, or any media item, ALWAYS include the actual attachment URL (the video/image file itself), not just a link to the Discord message that contains it. A message link doesn't answer \"show me the video\". Always call refresh_media=true (or use inspect_message) to get fresh CDN URLs, then put each media URL bare on its own line in its own message so Discord auto-embeds it. Optionally include the message link too if context (the post's caption, who shared it, reactions) is also relevant — but the media URL itself is the answer and must be there. send_message auto-refreshes CDN URLs.\n+**Media.** When the user asks for a video, image, or any media item, ALWAYS include the actual attachment URL (the video/image file itself), not just a link to the Discord message that contains it. A message link doesn't answer \"show me the video\". Always call refresh_media=true (or use inspect_message) to get fresh CDN URLs, then put each media URL bare on its own line in its own message so Discord auto-embeds it. Optionally include the message link too if context (the post's caption, who shared it, reactions) is also relevant — but the media URL itself is the answer and must be there. send_message auto-refreshes CDN URLs. For scheduled or failed social publishing work, inspect `social_publications` and `social_channel_routes` with `query_table`.\n@@\n-- Share to social media with `share_to_social`, including thread replies via `reply_to_tweet`; use the returned `tweet_url`/`tweet_id`, and if `already_shared` is true, report the existing `tweet_url`.\n+- Share to social media with `share_to_social`, including scheduled posts/replies/retweets. Use the returned `publication_id` for canonical tracking, and if `already_shared` is true, report the existing `tweet_url`.\n*** End Patch\n"

Include the `claim_due_social_publications` Postgres function that atomically selects and updates due rows (status='queued', scheduled_at <= now()) to status='processing' using `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`, returning claimed rows.\n- Modify `src/common/db_handler.py` \u2014 Add methods using **fluent Supabase client** (NOT raw SQL) for all CRUD: `create_social_publication()`, `get_social_publication_by_id()`, `get_social_publications_for_message()`, `list_social_publications()`, `mark_social_publication_processing()`, `mark_social_publication_succeeded()`, `mark_social_publication_failed()`, `mark_social_publication_cancelled()`.     "description": "Introduce SocialPublishService with publish_now/enqueue entrypoints and wrap X behind a provider interface.\n\n**Files to create/modify:**\n- Create `src/features/sharing/social_publish_service.py` \u2014 Central service with:\n  - `publish_now(request: SocialPublishRequest) -> SocialPublishResult`: Immediate execution path. Creates canonical publication record (status='processing'), delegates to provider, updates to succeeded/failed, performs legacy shared_posts compatibility write for primary non-reply posts, returns result with publication_id.\n  - `enqueue(request: SocialPublishRequest) -> SocialPublishResult`: Creates canonical record with status='queued' and scheduled_at, returns result with publication_id (no provider call yet).\n  - `execute_publication(publication_id)`: Internal method used by scheduler to execute a claimed publication.\n  - `delete_publication(publication_id)`: Loads canonical record, validates delete_supported, delegates to provider.\n  - Success path: persist canonical status \u2192 optional legacy shared_posts write \u2192 return result with publication_id + provider refs + delete_supported + announcement data.\n\n- Create `src/features/sharing/providers/__init__.py` and `src/features/sharing/providers/x_provider.py` \u2014 Provider interface and X implementation:\n  - Interface: `publish(request)`, `delete(publication)`, `normalize_target_ref(...)`\n  - X provider wraps existing `social_poster.post_tweet()` and `social_poster.delete_tweet()` \n  - Supports actions: post, reply, retweet. Delete supported for post and reply only; retweet explicitly marked delete_supported=False.\n\n- Modify `src/features/sharing/sharer.py` \u2014 Refactor `Sharer` to be request-building and orchestration code:\n  - Keep media download, title extraction, caption generation in Sharer\n  - `finalize_sharing()` builds a `SocialPublishRequest` and hands it to `SocialPublishService.publish_now()` instead of calling `post_tweet()` directly\n  - Move duplicate detection from in-memory `_successfully_shared` set to canonical DB lookups that are action-aware and caller-aware (primary non-reply posts dedupe, replies don't, reaction bridge doesn't inherit admin duplicate suppression)\n  - `send_tweet()` similarly routes through the service\n\n**Critical behavioral preservation:**\n- Admin replies default to text_only=True (callers-2 flag)\n- Reaction bridge does NOT inherit duplicate suppression (callers-3 flag)\n- First-share notification now receives publication_id from result",

    "description": "Add route resolution and scheduled worker lifecycle on the real load path.\n\n**Files to create/modify:**\n\n1. `src/common/server_config.py` \u2014 Add route resolution helper:\n   - `resolve_social_route(guild_id, channel_id, platform)`: Requires sharing_enabled via is_feature_enabled(), then resolves: exact channel route \u2192 parent channel route \u2192 guild default route\n   - For v1, guild-default routing is the only active path (settled decision `v1-routing-scope`); channel-specific routes can be seeded via direct DB inserts\n   - Explicit route/platform override only where admin tool provides one\n\n2. `src/features/sharing/sharing_cog.py` \u2014 Add scheduled worker:\n   - Use `discord.ext.tasks.loop` pattern (consistent with repo conventions)\n   - Worker runs on interval (e.g., 30s), calls db_handler.claim_due_social_publications(), executes each through SocialPublishService.execute_publication()\n   - **Critical: main.py instantiates SharingCog directly** (`SharingCog(bot, bot.db_handler)` then `await bot.add_cog(...)`) \u2014 worker startup MUST use cog_load/cog_unload lifecycle hooks, NOT rely on module-level setup() alone\n   - before_loop: wait_until_ready() gating\n   - cog_load: start the loop\n   - cog_unload: cancel/stop the loop\n   - Keep setup() aligned with direct-instantiation path for compatibility\n   - Bounded retries for transient provider failures; persist terminal states with publication_id, platform, action, and provider error in logs\n   - Normalize scheduled times to UTC before persistence; no natural-language time parsing\n\n3. `main.py` \u2014 Wire SocialPublishService into SharingCog:\n   - SharingCog needs access to SocialPublishService (constructed with db_handler and X provider)\n   - Ensure the service instance is accessible to admin tools and sharer via bot or cog reference",

      "description": "Add route resolution and scheduled worker lifecycle on the real load path.\n\n**Files to create/modify:**\n\n1. `src/common/server_config.py` \u2014 Add route resolution helper:\n   - `resolve_social_route(guild_id, channel_id, platform)`: Requires sharing_enabled via is_feature_enabled(), then resolves: exact channel route \u2192 parent channel route \u2192 guild default route\n   - For v1, guild-default routing is the only active path (settled decision `v1-routing-scope`); channel-specific routes can be seeded via direct DB inserts\n   - Explicit route/platform override only where admin tool provides one\n\n2. `src/features/sharing/sharing_cog.py` \u2014 Add scheduled worker:\n   - Use `discord.ext.tasks.loop` pattern (consistent with repo conventions)\n   - Worker runs on interval (e.g., 30s), calls db_handler.claim_due_social_publications(), executes each through SocialPublishService.execute_publication()\n   - **Critical: main.py instantiates SharingCog directly** (`SharingCog(bot, bot.db_handler)` then `await bot.add_cog(...)`) \u2014 worker startup MUST use cog_load/cog_unload lifecycle hooks, NOT rely on module-level setup() alone\n   - before_loop: wait_until_ready() gating\n   - cog_load: start the loop\n   - cog_unload: cancel/stop the loop\n   - Keep setup() aligned with direct-instantiation path for compatibility\n   - Bounded retries for transient provider failures; persist terminal states with publication_id, platform, action, and provider error in logs\n   - Normalize scheduled times to UTC before persistence; no natural-language time parsing\n\n3. `main.py` \u2014 Wire SocialPublishService into SharingCog:\n   - SharingCog needs access to SocialPublishService (constructed with db_handler and X provider)\n   - Ensure the service instance is accessible to admin tools and sharer via bot or cog reference",

Do not skip ahead.\n\n**Key gotchas:**\n\n1. **RPC is new to this codebase.** The `claim_due_social_publications` Postgres function must be deployed to Supabase before the scheduler works. Pytest is loading the suite, but the repo doesn’t have `pytest-asyncio`, so the async tests were being skipped under plain `@pytest.mark.asyncio`. I’m switching them to the installed `anyio` plugin and tightening one DB helper assertion so the tests validate the updated row instead of a stale copy.

def create_social_publication(self, data, guild_id=None):\n+        publication_id = \"pub-{0}\".format(len(self.rows) + 1)\n+        row = dict(data)\n+        row[\"publication_id\"] = publication_id\n+        row[\"guild_id\"] = guild_id or row.get(\"guild_id\")\n+        self.rows[publication_id] = row\n+        return row\n+\n+    def get_social_publication_by_id(self, retry_after=None):\n+        self.rows[publication_id][\"status\"] = \"processing\"\n+        return True\n+\n+    def mark_social_publication_succeeded(self, publication_id, guild_id=None, provider_ref=None, provider_url=None, delete_supported=None):\n+        row = self.rows[publication_id]\n+        row[\"status\"] = \"succeeded\"\n+        row[\"provider_ref\"] = provider_ref\n+        row[\"provider_url\"] = provider_url\n+        row[\"delete_supported\"] = delete_supported\n+        return True\n+\n+    def mark_social_publication_failed(self, publication_id, last_error, mark_social_publication_cancelled(self, publication_id, guild_id=None, last_error=None):\n+        self.rows[publication_id][\"status\"] = \"cancelled\"\n+        return True\n+\n+    def record_shared_post(self, **kwargs):\n+        self.shared_posts.append(kwargs)\n+        return True\n+\n+    def mark_shared_post_deleted(self, discord_message_id, platform, guild_id=None):\n+        self.deleted_shared_posts.append((discord_message_id, platform, guild_id))\n+        return True\n+\n+\n+def build_request(action=\"post\", scheduled_at=None, target_post_ref=None):\n+    return scheduled_at=scheduled_at,\n+        target_post_ref=target_post_ref,\n+        text=\"hello world\" if action != \"retweet\" else None,\n+        source_kind=\"admin_chat\",\n+        duplicate_policy={\"check_existing\": action == \"post\"},\n+        source_context=PublicationSourceContext(\n+            source_kind=\"admin_chat\",\n+            metadata={\"user_details\": request.duplicate_policy[\"check_existing\"] is True\n+\n+    immediate = await service.publish_now(request)\n+    assert immediate.success is True\n+    assert immediate.publication_id ==  assert provider.delete_calls == [\"pub-1\"]\n+    print(\"ok\")\n+\n+\n+asyncio.run(main())\n*** End Patch\n"
